//! Release-only storage outside the active row/index namespace.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#2-retain-by-default — moves, restorations and deletion evidence share activation's transaction.
use super::*;
use crate::schema::{ColumnDefault, ColumnType, materialize_optional_fields};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

pub(crate) const VERSION: &str = "snapback4-release-store-3";

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
struct Column {
    kind: ColumnType,
    default: Option<ColumnDefault>,
    authoritative: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    capture_source: Option<Table>,
}

pub(crate) fn enable(conn: &Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_narrow_rows(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,reason TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_tables(table_name TEXT PRIMARY KEY, definition TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_rows(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_columns(table_name TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(table_name,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_values(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,live_source INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,PRIMARY KEY(t,id,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_indexes(table_name TEXT NOT NULL,index_name TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(table_name,index_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_refs(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,invalid INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,PRIMARY KEY(t,id,column_name,parent_t,parent_id)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_parent ON _dormant_refs(parent_t,parent_id);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _release_events(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,at INTEGER NOT NULL,operation TEXT NOT NULL,release INTEGER,previous INTEGER,generation INTEGER NOT NULL,revision INTEGER NOT NULL,summary TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _release_event_items(event INTEGER NOT NULL,path TEXT NOT NULL,detail TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(event,path)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    if !live_cells_column(conn)? {
        // Published predecessors have captured-only cells. Defaulting them
        // to captured is conservative; shared definitions cannot prove a
        // particular retained cell ever had live authority.
        conn.execute_batch("ALTER TABLE _dormant_values ADD COLUMN live_source INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;")?;
    }
    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version' AND value!='snapback4-release-store-4'", [VERSION])?;
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn live_cells_column(conn:&Connection)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    Ok(conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM pragma_table_info('_dormant_values') WHERE name='live_source')",[],|r|r.get(0))?)
}

pub(super) fn enabled(tx: &Tx<'_>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(matches!(tx.meta("version")?.as_deref(),Some(VERSION|"snapback4-release-store-2"|super::backfills::VERSION)))
}

fn authoritative(table: &Table, column: &str) -> bool {
    !table.maintained.contains_key(column) && !table.engine_field(column)
}

pub(super) fn definition(tx: &Tx<'_>, name: &str, table: &Table, column: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let value = Column {
        kind: table.columns[column].clone(),
        default: table.defaults.get(column).cloned(),
        authoritative: authoritative(table, column),
        capture_source: table.engine_field(column).then(||table.clone()),
    };
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_columns VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(table_name,column_name) DO UPDATE SET definition=excluded.definition", params![name,column,canonical_json(&value)])?;
    Ok(())
}

fn references(kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) => references(inner, value, out),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(values)) => {
            for value in values {
                references(of, value, out);
            }
        }
        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => out.push((table.clone(), id.clone())),
        _ => {}
    }
}

fn rewrite_legacy_reference(
    kind: &ColumnType,
    value: &mut Value,
    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
) -> bool {
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => {
            let Some(next) = ids.get(table).and_then(|ids| ids.get(id)) else { return false };
            *id = next.clone();
            true
        }
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) => rewrite_legacy_reference(inner, value, ids),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(values)) =>
            values.iter_mut().fold(false, |changed, value| rewrite_legacy_reference(of, value, ids) || changed),
        _ => false,
    }
}

fn rewrite_legacy_row(
    table: &Table,
    row: &mut Value,
    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
) -> bool {
    let (Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields)) = row else { return false };
    table.columns.iter().fold(false, |changed, (column, kind)| {
        fields.get_mut(column).is_some_and(|value| rewrite_legacy_reference(kind, value, ids)) || changed
    })
}

/// Rewrite references retained outside the active namespace alongside their
/// active counterparts. References whose aggregate target was itself retired
/// have no map and retain the ordinary dormant-lifetime refusal on restore.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.003#3.0 — identity adoption covers every surviving typed reference, including dormant storage.
pub(crate) fn rewrite_legacy_maintained_references(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    if ids.values().all(BTreeMap::is_empty) || !enabled(tx)? { return Ok(0); }
    let mut rewritten = 0_u64;

    let cells = tx.conn().prepare(
        "SELECT v.t,v.id,v.column_name,v.data,c.definition FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog k ON k.num=v.t JOIN _dormant_columns c ON c.table_name=k.name AND c.column_name=v.column_name ORDER BY v.t,v.id,v.column_name"
    )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?, row.get::<_,String>(4)?)))?
        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    for (t, id, column, data, definition) in cells {
        let definition: Column = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        let mut value: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if rewrite_legacy_reference(&definition.kind, &mut value, ids) {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3 AND column_name=?4", params![canonical_json(&value),t,id,column])?;
            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
        }
    }

    let rows = tx.conn().prepare(
        "SELECT r.t,r.id,r.data,d.definition FROM _dormant_rows r JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=r.t JOIN _dormant_tables d ON d.table_name=c.name ORDER BY r.t,r.id"
    )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?)))?
        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    for (t, id, data, definition) in rows {
        let table: Table = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if rewrite_legacy_row(&table, &mut row, ids) {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_rows SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3", params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
        }
    }

    let narrowed = if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {
        tx.conn().prepare(
            "SELECT t,id,data,definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows ORDER BY t,id"
        )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?)))?
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?
    } else { Vec::new() };
    for (t, id, data, definition) in narrowed {
        let table: Table = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if rewrite_legacy_row(&table, &mut row, ids) {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_narrow_rows SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3", params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
        }
    }

    for (target, target_ids) in ids {
        if target_ids.is_empty() { continue; }
        let parent_t = tx.num(target)?;
        for (old, next) in target_ids {
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs(t,id,column_name,parent_t,parent_id,invalid) SELECT t,id,column_name,parent_t,?1,invalid FROM _dormant_refs WHERE parent_t=?2 AND parent_id=?3", params![next,parent_t,old])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2", params![parent_t,old])?;
        }
    }
    Ok(rewritten)
}

pub(super) fn capture_refs(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    t: i64,
    id: &str,
    column: &str,
    kind: &ColumnType,
    value: &Value,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    tx.conn().execute(
        "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
        params![t, id, column],
    )?;
    let mut refs = Vec::new();
    references(kind, value, &mut refs);
    for (parent, parent_id) in refs {
        add_reference(tx, t, id, column, &parent, &parent_id)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn add_reference(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    t: i64,
    id: &str,
    column: &str,
    parent: &str,
    parent_id: &str,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let parent_t = tx.num(parent)?;
    let invalid = !tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![parent_t,parent_id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?
        && !tx.conn().query_row(
            "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
            params![parent_t, parent_id],
            |r| r.get::<_, bool>(0),
        )?;
    tx.conn().execute(
        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6)",
        params![t, id, column, parent_t, parent_id, if invalid {2} else {0}],
    )?;
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn capture_through_parent(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    name: &str,
    table: &Table,
    id: &str,
    column: &str,
    row: &Value,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if let Some(through) = &table.through {
        if (through.fields.contains_key(column)
            || matches!(
                column,
                crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE | crate::schema::through::DEADLINE
            ))
            && let Ok(Value::Id(parent_id)) = row.field(&through.reference)
        {
            add_reference(tx, tx.num(name)?, id, column, &through.parent, parent_id)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

    column: &str,
    value: &Value,
    capture: bool,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let t = tx.num(name)?;

    // @ref LLP 3000.014.000#implementation-proof-clarifications — another row cannot overwrite a cell's live origin.

fn refuse(message: String) -> RuntimeError {
    RuntimeError::Structural {
        code: "E_ADOPT",
        message,
    }
}

pub(super) fn check_owner(tx: &Tx<'_>, name: &str, old: &Table, next: &Schema) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let Some(new) = next.table(name) else {
        return Ok(());
    };
    let evidence = || -> Result<String, RuntimeError> {
        let mut samples = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
        super::activation::walk(tx, tx.num(name)?, |id, _| {
            samples.add(id, "storage ownership");
            Ok(())
        })?;
        if samples.count == 0 {
            let t = tx.num(name)?;
            let count: i64 =
                tx.conn()
                    .query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1", [t], |r| {
                        r.get(0)
                    })?;
            let ids = tx
                .conn()
                .prepare("SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id LIMIT 5")?
                .query_map([t], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))?
                .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
            return Ok(format!(
                "{count} affected rows; sample ids [{}]",
                ids.iter()
                    .map(|i| super::adopt_refusals::bounded(i))
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join(", ")
            ));
        }
        Ok(samples.describe())
    };
    if new.ephemeral.is_some() {
        return Err(refuse(format!(
            "table {name}: {}; durable storage cannot become ephemeral; deploy without {name}, run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`, then deploy the ephemeral table",
            evidence()?
        )));
    }
    if next.maintain_for(name).is_some() {
        return Err(refuse(format!(
            "table {name}: {}; authoritative storage cannot become a derived table; rename the derived table, or retire {name}, run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`, then deploy",
            evidence()?
        )));
    }
    for column in old
        .columns
        .keys()
        .filter(|column| authoritative(old, column))
    {
        if new.columns.contains_key(column) && !authoritative(new, column) {
            return Err(refuse(format!(
                "column {name}.{column}: {}; authoritative values cannot become maintained/captured values; rename the new column, or retire it, run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`, then deploy",
                evidence()?
            )));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Restore all parents before checking any retained child's lifetime. Consume
/// only candidate-visible cells, so partial table restoration is reversible.
pub(super) fn transition(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    next: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    restore: bool,
    now:i64,
    report: &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport,
) -> Result<BTreeSet<String>, RuntimeError> {
    if !enabled(tx)? {
        return Ok(BTreeSet::new());
    }
    if !super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? || !live_cells_column(tx.conn())? {enable(tx.conn())?;}
    let whole: Vec<(String, String)> = tx
        .conn()
        .prepare("SELECT table_name,definition FROM _dormant_tables ORDER BY table_name")?
        .query_map([], |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?)))?
        .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
    let mut restoring = Vec::new();
    let mut retained_through = BTreeSet::new();
    for (name, text) in &whole {
        let old: Table =
            serde_json::from_str(text).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        check_owner(tx, name, &old, next)?;
        if next.tables.contains_key(name) {
            if old.through.is_some() {
                retained_through.insert(name.clone());
            }
            restoring.push((name.clone(), old));
        }
    }
    let cells:Vec<(String,String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT table_name,column_name,definition FROM _dormant_columns ORDER BY table_name,column_name")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    let mut reuse: Vec<String> = restoring.iter().map(|(n, _)| n.clone()).collect();
    for (name, column, text) in &cells {
        if let Some(table) = next.table(name).filter(|t| t.columns.contains_key(column)) {
            if column == crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE {
                retained_through.insert(name.clone());
            }
            let old: Column =
                serde_json::from_str(text).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
            if let Some(source)=&old.capture_source {
                if source.through.is_some() && source.through == table.through {retained_through.insert(name.clone());}
                else if source.through.as_ref().zip(table.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|(a,b)| (a.live.is_some() || b.live.is_some()) && a.reference==b.reference && a.parent==b.parent) { /* Restamp destination; live provenance never retains historical authority. */ }
                else if source.through.is_some() && table.through.is_some() {return Err(refuse(format!("{name}: retained engine captures belong to another read-through definition; restore the original definition before changing the audience")));}
            }
            if old.authoritative && !authoritative(table, column) {
                let mut samples = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
                let t = tx.num(name)?;
                let mut statement = tx.conn().prepare(
                    "SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 ORDER BY id",
                )?;
                for row in statement.query_map(params![t, column], |r| {
                    Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?))
                })? {
                    let (id, value) = row?;
                    samples.add(&id, &super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&value));
                }
                return Err(refuse(format!(
                    "column {name}.{column}: {}; dormant authoritative values cannot become maintained/captured; rename the new column, or run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive` while it is dormant, then deploy",
                    samples.describe()
                )));
            }
            if previous.and_then(|s|s.table(name)).is_none_or(|t|!t.columns.contains_key(column)) {reuse.push(format!("{name}.{column}"));}
        }
    }
    let indexes: Vec<(String, String)> = tx
        .conn()
        .prepare(
            "SELECT table_name,index_name FROM _dormant_indexes ORDER BY table_name,index_name",
        )?
        .query_map([], |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?)))?
        .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
    for (name, index) in &indexes {
        if next
            .table(name)
            .is_some_and(|t| t.indexes.contains_key(index))
        {
            reuse.push(format!("{name}.{index}"));
        }
    }
    if !restore && !reuse.is_empty() {
        let inventory = super::release_history::inventory(tx.conn(), None, usize::MAX)?;
        return Err(refuse(format!(
            "dormant names [{}] require restoration intent; retained storage: {}; proceed with `snapback4 deploy <dir> --restore-dormant`, or `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive` before fresh creation",
            reuse.join(", "),
            inventory.iter().map(|i|format!("{}: {} rows; sample ids [{}]",i.key,i.rows,i.sample_ids.join(", "))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ")
        )));
    }
    if let Some(previous) = previous {
        for (name, old) in previous
            .tables
            .iter()
            .filter(|(n, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none() && previous.maintain_for(n).is_none())
        {
            check_owner(tx, name, old, next)?;
            let candidate = next.table(name);
            let removed: Vec<_> = old
                .columns
                .keys()
                .filter(|c| candidate.is_none_or(|t| !t.columns.contains_key(*c)))
                .cloned()
                .collect();
            for column in &removed {
                definition(tx, name, old, column)?;
            }
            for (index, definition) in &old.indexes {
                if candidate.is_none_or(|t| !t.indexes.contains_key(index)) {

fn check_references(tx: &Tx<'_>, next: &Schema,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    for (name, table) in next.tables.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
        let t = tx.num(name)?;
        let mut bad = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
        let mut statement=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT d.id,d.column_name,d.parent_id,d.invalid FROM _dormant_refs d LEFT JOIN r p ON p.t=d.parent_t AND p.id=d.parent_id WHERE d.t=?1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r child WHERE child.t=d.t AND child.id=d.id) AND (d.invalid=1 OR (p.id IS NULL AND d.invalid!=2)) ORDER BY d.id,d.column_name")?;
        let mut rows = statement.query([t])?;
        while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
            let column: String = row.get(1)?;
            let id:String=row.get(0)?;
            if table.columns.contains_key(&column) && !super::authority_origin::obsolete_ref(tx,t,&id,&column,table,sources.get(name))? {
                bad.add(
                    &row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
                    &format!(
                        "{column} → parent {} (deleted/reused/missing)",
                        row.get::<_, String>(2)?
                    ),
                );
            }
        }
        if bad.count > 0 {
            return Err(refuse(format!(
                "table {name}: {}; dormant references cannot acquire another parent lifetime; keep the current release and repair the named references, or prune the dormant storage before restoring",
                bad.describe()
            )));
        }
        if let Some(through) = &table.through {
            {
                let mut bad = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
                super::activation_walk::walk_metered(tx,t,crate::rows::adoption::Work::DECODE,None,|id,row,meter| {
                    let restored: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
                        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
                        params![t, id],
                        |r| r.get(0),
                    )?;
                    if !restored {
                        return Ok(());
                    }
                    // The captured deadline belongs to one parent lifetime,
                    // even if an older schema allowed retargeting the active ref.
                    let parent_t = tx.num(&through.parent)?;
                    let current = row
                        .field(&through.reference)
                        .ok()
                        .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok());
                    let mut evidence=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT parent_t,parent_id,(SELECT name FROM _catalog WHERE num=parent_t) FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3")?;
                    for edge in evidence.query_map(
                        params![t, id, crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE],
                        |r| {
                            Ok((
                                r.get::<_, i64>(0)?,
                                r.get::<_, String>(1)?,
                                r.get::<_, String>(2)?,
                            ))
                        },
                    )? {
                        let (old_table, old_id, old_name) = edge?;
                        if old_table != parent_t || current != Some(old_id.as_str()) {
                            bad.add(
                                id,
                                &format!(
                                    "{}.{}: recorded {} [{}], current {} [{}]",
                                    name,
                                    through.reference,
                                    old_name,
                                    old_id,
                                    through.parent,
                                    current.unwrap_or("<missing>")
                                ),
                            );
                            return Ok(());
                        }
                    }
                    if let Ok(Value::Id(parent_id)) = row.field(&through.reference) {
                        if through.live.is_some() || crate::rows::Rows::restored_live_authority(tx,name,id)? {
                            let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![parent_t,parent_id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
                            meter.heap((bytes.unwrap_or(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(4096))?;
                        }
                        if let Some(parent) = tx.get(&through.parent, parent_id)? {
                            let parent =
                                crate::schema::row_image(&parent, &next.tables[&through.parent]);
                            // @ref LLP 3000.014.000#71-dormant-restoration-identities-are-a-relation — live policy restamps current inputs after preserving parent lifetime.
                            if through.live.is_none() && !crate::rows::Rows::restored_live_authority(tx,name,id)? && through.fields.iter().any(|(capture, field)| {
                                row.field(capture)
                                    .ok()
                                    .is_some_and(|value| Some(value) != parent.field(field).ok())
                            }) {
                                bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} parent {parent_id}: captured audience/identity changed",name,through.reference));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Ok(())
                })?;
                if bad.count > 0 {
                    return Err(refuse(format!(
                        "table {name}: {}; restore cannot rebind historical audience/deadline captures; restore the original parent reference and audience under the current release, then `snapback4 rollback <dir> --to N`, or explicitly `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`",
                        bad.describe()
                    )));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn delete(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str, name: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    super::backfills::delete(tx,t,id)?;
    if !enabled(tx)? {
        return Ok(());
    }
    tx.conn().execute(
        "UPDATE _dormant_refs SET invalid=1 WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2 AND invalid=0",
        params![t, id],
    )?;
    let narrowed=super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())?;
    let home=if narrowed {" OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?4 AND id=?2)"} else {""};
    tx.conn().execute(&format!("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?3) WHERE home_table=?1 AND home_id=?2 AND (home_column IN (SELECT column_name FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?4 AND id=?2){home})"),params![name,id,crate::now_ms(),t])?;
    tx.conn().execute(
        "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
        params![t, id],
    )?;
    tx.conn().execute(
        "DELETE FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
        params![t, id],
    )?;
    if narrowed {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;}
    tx.conn().execute(
        "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
        params![t, id],
    )?;
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn holds_home(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &str, column: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    if !enabled(tx)? {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    let narrowed=if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {" OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON n.t=c.num WHERE c.name=?1)"}else{""};
    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(&format!("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_tables WHERE table_name=?1) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_columns WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2){narrowed}"),params![table,column],|r|r.get(0))?)
}

/// Fetch only destination cells on live transitions, after paying their full
/// serialized/decoded coexistence in the owning activation page/row account.
pub(super) fn restored_values(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,table:&Table,meter:&mut crate::meter::Meter)->Result<Vec<(String,String)>,RuntimeError> {
    if !super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
        return Ok(tx.conn().prepare_cached("SELECT column_name,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?.query_map(params![t,id],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?);
    }
    let mut values=Vec::new();
    for column in table.columns.keys() {
        let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
        if let Some(bytes)=bytes {
            meter.heap((bytes as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(1024+column.len() as u64*8))?;
            let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0))?;
            values.push((column.clone(),data));
        }
    }
    Ok(values)
}

/// Narrowed forward correspondence has already run when this is called.
/// Record cell-derived live provenance before consuming any source evidence.
pub(super) fn mark_live_column(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,column:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    if super::live_activation::active(tx)? && super::authority_origin::mode(tx,t,id)? != Some(1) {
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'live-authority',?1,'',?2 WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND live_source=1)",params![t,id,column])?;
    }Ok(())
}

//! Release row lifetimes whose present values cannot inhabit the selected type.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#91-per-row-narrowed-value-dormancy — hide a reference-closed population; never coerce values.
use super::*;
use crate::schema::{ColumnType, Constraint};

pub(super) fn present(conn: &Connection) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(conn.query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_dormant_narrow_rows')",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}

fn incompatible(tx:&Tx<'_>,schema: &Schema, name: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    incompatible_except(tx,schema,name,row,&[])
}
fn incompatible_except(tx:&Tx<'_>,schema: &Schema,name:&str,row:&Value,authority:&[String])->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    let table = &schema.tables[name];
    for (column,kind) in &table.columns {
        if table.engine_field(column) || table.maintained.contains_key(column) || authority.contains(column) {continue;}
        if let Ok(value)=row.field(column) {
            if incompatible_column(tx, table, name, column, kind, value)? { return Ok(true); }
        }
    }
    Ok(false)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — the warning and activation share the value predicate, including ledger-backed assets.
pub(super) fn incompatible_column(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &Table, name: &str, column: &str, kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    if crate::engine::validate::validate_column(name, column, kind, Some(value)).is_err() { return Ok(true); }
    // @ref LLP 3000.038#1-the-type — asset facts belong to the ledger, including optional/list leaves.
    match super::activation::validate_assets(tx, kind, Some(value)) {
        Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE" | "E_ASSET_TOO_LARGE", .. }) => return Ok(true),
        Err(error) => return Err(error),
        Ok(()) => (),
    }
    Ok(table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { field, min, format }
        if field == column && matches!(value, Value::String(s)
            if crate::engine::validate::validate_text(field, s, *min, *format).is_err()))))
}

fn refs(kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), v) => refs(inner, v, out),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(vs)) => {
            for v in vs {
                refs(of, v, out);
            }
        }
        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => out.push((table.clone(), id.clone())),
        _ => {}
    }
}

pub(super) fn transition(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    next: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    now: i64,
    restored_images: bool,
    source_definitions: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
    authority_sources: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    retire_sources(tx,next,previous,now)?;
    let have_dormant: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows)",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?;
    let type_change = previous.is_some_and(|old| {
        next.tables.iter().any(|(name, t)| {
            old.table(name).is_some_and(|p| {
                t.columns
                    .iter()
                    .any(|(c, k)| p.columns.get(c).is_some_and(|old| old != k))
                    || t.constraints
                        .iter()
                        .filter(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { .. }))
                        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                        != p.constraints
                            .iter()
                            .filter(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { .. }))
                            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            })
        })
    });
    if !have_dormant && !type_change && !restored_images {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let mut newly_retained = 0u64;
    // Stream on the primary key. Do not retain a store-sized list of images.
    restore_images(tx,next,now,source_definitions,authority_sources,false)?;
    tx.clear_activation_scan();
    // References to a hidden row hide their dependents, including memberships.
    loop {
        let mut removed = 0u64;
        for (name, table) in next
            .tables
            .iter()
            .filter(|(n, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(n).is_none())
        {
            let t = tx.num(name)?;
            super::activation::walk(tx, t, |id, mut row| {
                if let Some(source)=authority_sources.get(name) {
                    if crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::retires_image(table,source,&row,now) {
                        tx.delete(name,source,&row)?;
                        return Ok(());
                    }
                }
                crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(
                    &mut row,
                    previous.and_then(|s| s.table(name)).unwrap_or(table),
                );
                let mut hidden = incompatible(tx,next, name, &row)?;
                for (column, kind) in &table.columns {
                    let mut edges = Vec::new();
                    if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
                        refs(kind, value, &mut edges);
                    }
                    for (parent, parent_id) in edges {
                        let missing = next.table(&parent).is_none()
                            || (!tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![tx.num(&parent)?,parent_id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?
                                && reserved(tx, tx.num(&parent)?, &parent_id)?);
                        if missing {
                            hidden = true;
                        }
                    }
                }
                if !hidden {
                    return Ok(());
                }
                let retained: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
                    "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
                    params![t, id],
                    |r| r.get(0),
                )?;
                if !retained {
                    newly_retained += 1;
                    let old = authority_sources.get(name).unwrap_or(table);
                    tx.conn().execute(
                        "INSERT INTO _dormant_narrow_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",
                        params![
                            t,
                            id,
                            canonical_json(&row),
                            canonical_json(old),
                            "type or dormant reference"
                        ],
                    )?;
                    for (column, kind) in &old.columns {
                        // Retained cells/whole rows already captured a lifetime; moving them must not erase deletion evidence.
                        let captured:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0))?;
                        if captured {
                            continue;
                        }
                        if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
                            if !super::authority_origin::live_input(old,column) {super::dormant::capture_refs(tx, t, id, column, kind, value)?;}
                        }
                        super::dormant::capture_through_parent(tx, name, old, id, column, &row)?;
                        // Nominal keep references may already be dangling. They have no bound lifetime.
                    }
                }
                tx.conn()
                    .execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2", params![t, id])?;
                removed += 1;
                Ok(())
            })?;
            tx.clear_activation_scan();
        }
        if removed == 0 {
            break;
        }
    }
    // Consume only rows that survived the closed population, not hidden dependents.
    let restored:u64=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id)",[],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64))?;
    if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'narrowed',n.t,'',n.id FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id)",[])?;
    } else {
    let restored_rows=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT c.name,n.id FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id)")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,String>(0)?,r.get::<_,String>(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    let mut restored_tables:std::collections::BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    for (name,id) in restored_rows {restored_tables.entry(name).or_default().push(id);}
    for (name, ids) in restored_tables {
        tx.set_meta(&format!("activation_narrowed_restored:{name}"),&ids.len().to_string())?;
        tx.set_meta(&format!("activation_narrowed_restored_ids:{name}"),&serde_json::to_string(&ids).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?)?;
    }
    }
    tx.set_meta("activation_narrowed_retained", &newly_retained.to_string())?;
    tx.set_meta("activation_narrowed_restored", &restored.to_string())?;
    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_dormant_narrow_rows.t AND r.id=_dormant_narrow_rows.id)",[])?;
    if newly_retained > 0 || restored > 0 {
        tx.set_meta("activation_population", "true")?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn reserved(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    if !present(tx.conn())? {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
        params![t, id],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}

pub(super) fn check_write(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    name: &str,
    _table: &Table,
    row: &Value,
    old: Option<&Value>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if !present(tx.conn())? {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?;
    let conflict = old.is_none() && reserved(tx, tx.num(name)?, id)?;
    if conflict {
        return Err(RuntimeError::Rule {
            table: name.into(),
            effect: if old.is_some() {
                crate::schema::Effect::Update
            } else {
                crate::schema::Effect::Insert
            },
        });
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn retire_sources(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,previous:Option<&Schema>,now:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    let backfilling=!next.backfills.is_empty() || super::backfills::has_evidence(tx)?;
    // An expired predecessor must not be archived and later revived without its lifetime.
    if let Some(previous) = previous {
        let retirement = crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::new(next, previous, now);
        for (name, old) in &previous.tables {
            if next.tables.contains_key(name) && (retirement.affects(name) || backfilling) {
                super::activation::walk(tx, tx.num(name)?, |_, row| {
                    if retirement.retires(name, &row) || backfilling && old.expired(&row,now) {
                        tx.delete(name, old, &row)?;
                    }
                    Ok(())
                })?;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

// Restore retained source images before backfill evaluation. Candidate-owned
// output cells are checked after transformation, so a same-column enum rename
// does not preclude its own source image from entering the snapshot.
pub(super) fn restore_images(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,now:i64,source_definitions:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,authority_sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,for_backfills:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    let rewritten=if for_backfills {super::backfills::columns(tx,next)?}else{Default::default()};
    let mut cursor = (0i64, String::new());
    loop {
        let item: Option<(i64,String,i64,i64)> = tx.conn().query_row(
            "SELECT t,id,octet_length(data),octet_length(definition) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE (t,id)>(?1,?2) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_dormant_narrow_rows.t AND r.id=_dormant_narrow_rows.id) ORDER BY t,id LIMIT 1",
            params![cursor.0,cursor.1],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?,r.get(3)?))).optional()?;
        let Some((t,id,data_bytes,definition_bytes))=item else {break};
        // Retain one admitted provenance/image unit through decode, overlays,
        // reconciliation and encoding. Never own its blobs before this bound.
        let mut admission=crate::meter::Meter::new(crate::meter::Ceilings::job(),crate::meter::Cut::Abort);
        if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
            admission.heap((data_bytes as u64).saturating_add(definition_bytes as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
        }
        let (data,definition):(String,String)=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data,definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?;
        cursor = (t, id.clone());
        let name: String =
            tx.conn()
                .query_row("SELECT name FROM _catalog WHERE num=?1", [t], |r| r.get(0))?;
        let Some(table) = next
            .table(&name)
            .filter(|t| t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(&name).is_none())
        else {
            continue;
        };
        let old: Table =
            serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        super::dormant::check_owner(tx, &name, &old, next)?;
        let mut row: Value =
            serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&old);
        let mut authority=crate::engine::through_activation::manual_authority_fields(next,&name,&old);
        if for_backfills { authority.extend(rewritten.get(&name).into_iter().flatten().cloned()); }
        if incompatible_except(tx,next,&name,&row,&authority)? {
            continue;
        }
        // Retained source deadlines precede projection, including removal of read-through itself.
        if crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::retires_image(table,&old,&row,now) || for_backfills && old.expired(&row,now) {
            tx.delete(&name,&old,&row)?;
            continue;
        }
        // Actual deletion/reuse never recaptures a different parent lifetime.
        let invalid_columns = tx
            .conn()
            .prepare_cached(
                "SELECT column_name FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND invalid=1",
            )?
            .query_map(params![t, id], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))?
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
        let mut invalid=false;
        for c in &invalid_columns {
            if for_backfills && next.backfills.iter().any(|f| f.table==name && f.inputs.contains_key(c)) {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Structural{code:"E_ADOPT",message:format!("backfill historical reference {name}.{c} on {id} lost its parent lifetime")});
            }
            if table.columns.contains_key(c) && !super::authority_origin::obsolete_ref(tx,t,&id,c,table,Some(&old))? {invalid=true;}
        }
        if invalid {
            continue;
        }
        if super::authority_origin::live_row(tx,t,&id,&old,table)? {
            admission.heap(crate::engine::through_activation::restored_parent_scratch(next,source_definitions,&name,&old).saturating_add(crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table).saturating_mul(4)))?;
        }
        super::authority_origin::record(tx,t,&id,&old)?;
        super::authority_origin::validate(tx,next,authority_sources,&name,&old,&row,now,true)?;
        crate::engine::through_activation::forward_image(tx,next,authority_sources,&name,&old,&row,now)?;
        if old.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some()) {tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored(kind,t,col,id) VALUES('live-authority',?1,'',?2)",params![t,id])?;}
        for column in old.columns.keys() {
            if !table.columns.contains_key(column) {
                super::dormant::definition(tx, &name, &old, column)?;
                if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
                    super::dormant::save_cell(tx, &name, &old, &id, column, value, false)?;
                }
            }
        }
        if !for_backfills { if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row {
            fields.retain(|c, _| table.columns.contains_key(c));
        }}
        let values=super::dormant::restored_values(tx,t,&id,table,&mut admission)?;
        super::authority_origin::overlay(tx,t,&id,Some(&old),table,&mut row,values)?;
        if old.through.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|e.live.is_some()) {
            for column in table.columns.keys() {super::dormant::mark_live_column(tx,t,&id,column)?;}
        }
        crate::engine::through_activation::manual_image(tx,next,&name,&old,&mut row,now)?;
        if if for_backfills {incompatible_except(tx,next,&name,&row,&authority)?}else{incompatible(tx,next,&name,&row)?} {continue;}
        if !for_backfills {crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);}
        // Missing required new fields are a genuine backfill refusal, not dormancy.
        tx.conn().execute(
            "INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",
            params![t, id, canonical_json(&row)],
        )?;
    }
    tx.clear_activation_scan();
    Ok(())
}

//! Database-authoritative release history and explicit dormant pruning.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#4-explicit-pruning-and-audit — no implicit collector or unaudited data loss.
use super::*;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DormantItem {
    /// Stable cursor; column/index names may coincide.
    pub key: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub kind: String,
    pub action: String,
    pub rows: u64,
    pub bytes: u64,
    pub asset_bytes: u64,
    /// Internal audit distinction: zero standalone cells may still be covered by a dormant row.
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub(crate) definition_only: bool,
    pub sample_ids: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub before_rows:Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub after_rows:Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub restored_rows:Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub before_sample_ids:Option<Vec<String>>,
}

impl DormantItem {
    pub fn unit(&self) -> &'static str {
        match (self.kind.as_str(), self.rows) { ("backfill", 1) => "preimage", ("backfill", _) => "preimages", ("column", 1) => "cell", ("column", _) => "cells", ("index", 1) => "entry", ("index", _) => "entries", (_, 1) => "row", _ => "rows" }
    }
}

impl Store {
    /// Values presently in active rows, independently of table population or other columns.
    pub fn rollback_incompatible_values(&self, current: &Schema, target: &Schema, name: &str, column: &str) -> Result<(u64, Vec<String>), RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.authority_transaction()?;
        let table = &target.tables[name];
        let kind = &table.columns[column];
        if table.engine_field(column) || table.maintained.contains_key(column) { return Ok((0, Vec::new())); }
        let mut count = 0;
        let mut samples = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        let mut statement = tx.conn().prepare("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=(SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1) ORDER BY id")?;
        for data in statement.query_map([name], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))? {
            let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data?).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
            crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, &current.tables[name]);
            if let Ok(value) = row.field(column)
                && super::narrowed::incompatible_column(&tx, table, name, column, kind, value)? {
                count += 1;
                if samples.len() < 5 {
                    let text = match value { Value::String(s) => format!("'{}'", s.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('\'', "\\'").replace('\n', "\\n").replace('\r', "\\r")), _ => canonical_json(value) };
                    samples.insert(super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&text));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok((count, samples.into_iter().collect()))
    }
}

fn sql_number(n: u64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
    i64::try_from(n).map_err(|_| {
        RuntimeError::Generation("release counter exceeds SQLite integer range".into())
    })
}

pub(super) fn present(conn: &Connection) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(conn.query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE type='table' AND name='_dormant_tables')",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}
pub(crate) fn inventory(
    conn: &Connection,
    after: Option<&str>,
    limit: usize,
) -> Result<Vec<DormantItem>, RuntimeError> {
    if !present(conn)? {
        return Ok(Vec::new());
    }
    let narrowed = if super::narrowed::present(conn)? {
        " UNION ALL SELECT c.name,'rows',c.name,NULL FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t GROUP BY n.t "
    } else {""};
    let backfills=super::backfills::inventory_sql(conn)?;
    let query=format!("SELECT path,kind,table_name,column_name FROM (
        SELECT table_name AS path,'table' AS kind,table_name,NULL AS column_name FROM _dormant_tables
        UNION ALL SELECT table_name||'.'||column_name,'column',table_name,column_name FROM _dormant_columns c WHERE table_name NOT IN (SELECT table_name FROM _dormant_tables) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog t ON t.num=v.t WHERE t.name=c.table_name AND v.column_name=c.column_name)
        UNION ALL SELECT table_name||'.'||index_name,'index',table_name,index_name FROM _dormant_indexes {narrowed} {backfills})
        WHERE (?1 IS NULL OR kind||':'||path>?1) ORDER BY kind||':'||path LIMIT ?2");
    let mut statement=conn.prepare(&query)?;
    let items: Vec<(String, String, String, Option<String>)> = statement
        .query_map(
            params![after, i64::try_from(limit).unwrap_or(i64::MAX)],
            |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?, r.get(2)?, r.get(3)?)),
        )?
        .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for (path, kind, name, column) in items {
        let t: Option<i64> = conn
            .query_row("SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1", [&name], |r| {
                r.get(0)
            })
            .optional()?;
        let Some(t) = t else { continue };
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — dormant row images cover their retained cells; standalone cells have active owners.
        let (mut count,mut bytes):(u64,u64)=match kind.as_str(){
            "backfill"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(inputs)+octet_length(before_image)+octet_length(after_image)),0) FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE t=?1 AND definition=?2",params![t,column],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            "table"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(data)),0) FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1",[t],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            "rows"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(data)),0) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1",[t],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            "column"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(data)),0) FROM _dormant_values v WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id)",params![t,column],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            _=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),0 FROM (SELECT id FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1)",[t],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
        };
        if matches!(kind.as_str(), "table" | "rows") {
            let images = if kind == "table" { "_dormant_rows" } else { "_dormant_narrow_rows" };
            bytes += conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(v.id)+octet_length(v.data)),0) FROM _dormant_values v WHERE v.t=?1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {images} d WHERE d.t=v.t AND d.id=v.id)"), [t], |r| r.get::<_, i64>(0))? as u64;
        }

            "backfill" => "SELECT id FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE t=?1 AND definition=?2 ORDER BY id LIMIT 5",
            "rows" => "SELECT id FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND ?2 IS NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT 5",
            "table" => "SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND ?2 IS NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT 5",
            "column" => {
                "SELECT id FROM _dormant_values v WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id) ORDER BY id LIMIT 5"
            }
            _ => {
                "SELECT id FROM (SELECT id FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1) WHERE ?2 IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT 5"
            }
        };
        let mut ids = conn
            .prepare(query)?
            .query_map(params![t, column], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))?
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?
            .into_iter()
            .map(|id| super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&id))
            .collect();
        if kind == "index" {
            (count, ids) = index_entries(conn, t, &name, column.as_deref().unwrap())?;
        }
        let asset_bytes = if kind == "index" || kind == "backfill" {
            0
        } else {
            let selector=match kind.as_str(){
                "rows"=>"EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=?3 AND n.id=_assets.home_id)",
                "column"=>"EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN r ON r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id WHERE v.t=?3 AND v.id=_assets.home_id AND v.column_name=_assets.home_column)",
                _=>"EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows d WHERE d.t=?3 AND d.id=_assets.home_id)",
            };
            conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(bytes),0) FROM _assets WHERE home_table=?1 AND (?2 IS NULL OR home_column=?2) AND released_at IS NULL AND {selector}"),params![name,if kind=="table" || kind=="rows" {None}else{column.as_deref()},t],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64))?
        };
        let definition_only = if kind == "column" {
            !conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2)", params![t, column], |r| r.get::<_, bool>(0))?
        } else { count == 0 };
        out.push(DormantItem {
            key: format!("{kind}:{path}"),
            path,
            kind,
            action: "dormant".into(),
            rows: count,
            bytes,
            asset_bytes,
            definition_only,
            sample_ids: ids,
            before_rows:None,after_rows:None,restored_rows:None,before_sample_ids:None,
        });
    }
    Ok(out)
}

/// Retained definitions represent logical entries, rebuilt on restoration.
/// A words index has zero or many entries per image; a row count is not its size.
fn index_entries(conn: &Connection, t: i64, table: &str, index: &str) -> Result<(u64, Vec<String>), RuntimeError> {
    let definition: String = conn.query_row("SELECT definition FROM _dormant_indexes WHERE table_name=?1 AND index_name=?2", params![table, index], |r| r.get(0))?;
    let index: crate::schema::Index = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
    let schema: Option<String> = conn.query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')", [], |r| r.get(0)).optional()?;
    let schema: Option<Schema> = schema.map(|s| serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))).transpose()?;
    let query = if super::narrowed::present(conn)? {
        "SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id"
    } else { "SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id" };
    let mut count = 0;
    let mut samples = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    let mut statement = conn.prepare(query)?;
    for image in statement.query_map([t], |r| Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?)))? {
        let (id, data) = image?;
        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if let Some(table) = schema.as_ref().and_then(|s| s.table(table)) { crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table); }
        for cell in conn.prepare("SELECT column_name,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?.query_map(params![t, id], |r| Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?)))? {
            let (column, value) = cell?;
            if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row {
                fields.entry(column).or_insert(serde_json::from_str(&value).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?);
            }
        }
        let entries = crate::rows::index_keys(&index, &row, &id).len() as u64;
        count += entries;
        if entries > 0 && samples.len() < 5 { samples.insert(super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&id)); }
    }
    Ok((count, samples.into_iter().collect()))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#91-per-row-narrowed-value-dormancy — only rows surviving every activation phase count as restored.
pub(crate) fn finalize_restorations(tx:&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    if super::live_activation::active(tx)? { return super::live_activation::finalize(tx); }
    let entries=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT key,value FROM _meta WHERE key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored_ids:%'")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,String>(0)?,r.get::<_,String>(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    let mut narrowed=0u64;
    for (key,ids) in entries {
        let (prefix,path)=key.split_once(':').expect("activation restoration key");
        let table=if prefix=="activation_column_restored_ids" {path.split_once('.').expect("column path").0} else {path};
        let count:u64=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?2))",params![tx.num(table)?,ids],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64))?;
        tx.set_meta(&format!("{}:{path}",prefix.trim_end_matches("_ids")),&count.to_string())?;
        if prefix=="activation_narrowed_restored_ids" {narrowed+=count;}
    }
    tx.set_meta("activation_narrowed_restored",&narrowed.to_string())?;
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn event(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    operation: &str,
    release: Option<u64>,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    summary: serde_json::Value,
    items: &[DormantItem],
) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    let revision = tx
        .meta("release_revision")?
        .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0)
        .checked_add(1)
        .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Generation("release revision exhausted".into()))?;
    tx.set_meta("release_revision", &revision.to_string())?;
    let generation = tx
        .meta("generation")?
        .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1);
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _release_events(at,operation,release,previous,generation,revision,summary) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6,?7)",params![crate::now_ms(),operation,release.map(sql_number).transpose()?,previous.map(sql_number).transpose()?,sql_number(generation)?,sql_number(revision)?,summary.to_string()])?;
    let id = tx.conn().last_insert_rowid();
    for item in items {
        tx.conn().execute(
            "INSERT INTO _release_event_items VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",
            params![id, item.key, canonical_json(item)],
        )?;
    }
    Ok(revision)
}

impl Store {
    pub fn dormant_inventory(
        &mut self,
        after: Option<&str>,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> Result<Vec<DormantItem>, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.read_transaction()?;
        let items = inventory(tx.conn(), after, limit)?;
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(items)
    }
    /// One snapshot: current identity, bounded history and storage inventories.
    pub fn release_status(
        &mut self,
        after: u64,
        items_after: Option<&str>,
        event_id: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<serde_json::Value, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.read_transaction()?;
        let mut events = Vec::new();
        let mut inventory_page = Vec::<serde_json::Value>::new();
        if present(tx.conn())? {
            let mut s=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT id,at,operation,release,previous,generation,revision,summary FROM _release_events WHERE id>?1 ORDER BY id LIMIT 16")?;
            let mut rows = s.query([sql_number(after)?])?;
            while let Some(r) = rows.next()? {
                events.push(serde_json::json!({"id":r.get::<_,i64>(0)?,"at":r.get::<_,i64>(1)?,"operation":r.get::<_,String>(2)?,"release":r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(3)?,"previous":r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(4)?,"generation":r.get::<_,i64>(5)?,"revision":r.get::<_,i64>(6)?,"summary":serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&r.get::<_,String>(7)?).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?}));
            }
            if let Some(event) = event_id {
                let mut s=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT detail FROM _release_event_items WHERE event=?1 AND (?2 IS NULL OR path>?2) ORDER BY path LIMIT 16")?;
                for item in s.query_map(params![sql_number(event)?, items_after], |r| {
                    r.get::<_, String>(0)
                })? {
                    inventory_page.push(
                        serde_json::from_str(&item?)
                            .map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?,
                    );
                }
            } else {
                inventory_page = inventory(tx.conn(), items_after, 16)?
                    .into_iter()
                    .map(|i| serde_json::to_value(i).unwrap())
                    .collect();
            }
        }
        let workers:Vec<(String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT key,value FROM _meta WHERE key='worker:status' OR key LIKE 'worker:status:%' ORDER BY key LIMIT 16")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
        let previous_active: Option<u64> = if present(tx.conn())? {tx.conn().query_row("SELECT previous FROM _release_events WHERE release IS NOT NULL AND previous != release AND operation != 'prune' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",[],|r|r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(0)).optional()?.flatten().map(|n|n as u64)} else {None};
        let result = serde_json::json!({"previous_active":previous_active,"release":tx.meta("release")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()),"revision":tx.meta("release_revision")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),"generation":tx.meta("generation")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(1),"native_digest":tx.meta("release_native_digest")?,"events_after":events.last().map(|e|e["id"].clone()),"events":events,"items_after":inventory_page.last().map(|i|i["key"].clone()),"items":inventory_page,"event":event_id,"workers":workers});
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(result)
    }
    pub fn prune_dormant(
        &mut self,
        confirm: bool,
        expected_revision: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<serde_json::Value, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.transaction()?;
        let held = tx
            .meta("release_revision")?
            .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0);
        if expected_revision.is_some_and(|n| n != held) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation(
                "release revision changed; run releases and retry prune".into(),
            ));
        }
        let items = inventory(tx.conn(), None, usize::MAX)?;
        if !confirm {
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(
                serde_json::json!({"pruned":false,"items":items,"rewrite":"snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive"}),
            );
        }
        if !present(tx.conn())? {
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(serde_json::json!({"pruned":true,"items":[],"revision":held}));
        }
        // Release only dormant homes; active application data is untouched.
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?1) WHERE home_table IN (SELECT table_name FROM _dormant_tables) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=v.t WHERE c.name=_assets.home_table AND v.column_name=_assets.home_column AND v.id=_assets.home_id)",[crate::now_ms()])?;
        if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?1) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t WHERE c.name=_assets.home_table AND n.id=_assets.home_id)",[crate::now_ms()])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=_dormant_values.t AND n.id=_dormant_values.id)",[])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows",[])?;
        }
        let revision = event(
            &tx,
            "prune",
            tx.meta("release")?.and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()),
            None,
            serde_json::json!({"dropped_items":items.len(),"bytes":items.iter().map(|i|i.bytes).sum::<u64>(),"asset_bytes":items.iter().map(|i|i.asset_bytes).sum::<u64>()}),
            &items,
        )?;
        let event_id = tx.conn().last_insert_rowid();
        super::backfills::prune(&tx)?;
        tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _dormant_values;DELETE FROM _dormant_rows;DELETE FROM _dormant_refs;DELETE FROM _dormant_columns;DELETE FROM _dormant_indexes;DELETE FROM _dormant_tables;")?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(serde_json::json!({"pruned":true,"items":items,"revision":revision,"event":event_id}))
    }
}

fn cross_reference_existence_is_evaluated_before_any_table_output_changes(){

fn removal_of_last_opening_boundary_keeps_the_durable_retirement_floor() {

  let(s,p)=compile(&next);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,requested).unwrap();

 }

#[test]
fn reentry_restores_shared_snapshots_for_arguments_and_point_reads() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for later in [false,true] {
  let copied=twice.replace("  n: int","  copied: int\n  lookedUp: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items copy(n: int, id: items):\n  {copied:n, lookedUp:(items[id] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));
  if later {adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();}
  adopt(&mut e,&copied,3).unwrap();let expected=if later {6}else{3};
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","copied"),Value::Int(expected));
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&copied,3).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","copied"),Value::Int(expected));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","lookedUp"),Value::Int(expected));
 }
}
